P01 P16 P19 P21 :מנופים· N25 N23 N22 N21 N20 N11 N10 N07 :נישות
מוצר  אותו  של  מאקרו  תקריב +  שקוף  רקע  על  תלת־ממדי  רינדור  בסגנון  מוצר  תמונת:  נכסים· gsap 3.15 :ספריות
E1-02 E1-07 E1-04 :עם  ביחד  לא· E1-13 E1-68 E1-60 :עם  משתלב
מעליה.  ההתחלה  בקצה  נחתכת,  מהרקע  אחד  בגוון  רק  בהירה,  המסך  כל  של  בגודל  המותג  מילת:  ושקט  מונוכרומטי  פתיחה  מסך  .רואים  מה
בפינה.  רך  גלולה  וכפתור  קטן  אחד  משפט,  שורות  בשלוש  צפופה  כותרת  המוצר  וליד —  רך  מגע  צל  עם  סטודיו  בצילום  אחד  חפץ —  המוצר
המילה  על  המותג  בצבע  אחת  ונקודה,  שקט  ברצף  נכנס  הכול  בטעינה.  ובתחתית  בקצה  חתוך,  מהמוצר  מזהה  פרט  של  תקריב  יושב  הנגדית

והמוצר(  הצדדים  משני  יוצאת,  64vw) המילה  עם  58svh של  ויזואלי  בלוק:  אנכית  מסתדר  המבנה  במובייל  .RTLו־  מופחתת  תנועה,  מובייל

element.css
/* E1-01 · Ghost word product hero. Logical properties only: mirrors itself in RTL and LTR. */
.gw {
  --surface: #F3EFE6;
  --ink: #1A2420;
  --ink-soft: #2E3632;
  --ink-muted: #5B635E;
  --accent: #7E5608;      /* CTA label: passes 4.5:1 on --surface */
  --accent-dot: #C8941C;  /* the one bright accent detail on the ghost word */
  --ghost: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.6);
  --display: 'Heebo Variable' , 'Rubik Variable' , system-ui, sans-serif;
  --ease: cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);
  position: relative;
  min-height: 100svh;
  max-height: 940px;

  overflow: hidden;
  overflow-x: clip;
  isolation: isolate;
  color: var(--ink);
  font-family: var(--display);
  background:
    radial-gradient(ellipse 34% 42% at 78% 46%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.6), transparent 70%),
    linear-gradient(180deg, #F6F3EC 0%, #EDE8DD 100%);
}
.gw:dir(ltr) { background:
    radial-gradient(ellipse 34% 42% at 22% 46%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.6), transparent 70%),
    linear-gradient(180deg, #F6F3EC 0%, #EDE8DD 100%); }
/* Layer 0 — the giant tone-on-tone word, cropped by the start edge */
.gw__ghost {
  position: absolute;
  z-index: 0;
  inset-inline-start: -4vw;
  top: 50%;
  translate: 0 -48%;
  font-weight: 800;
  font-size: clamp(260px, 44vw, 900px); /* JS refits it to the hero width */
  line-height: 0.8;
  letter-spacing: -0.04em;
  white-space: nowrap;
  color: var(--ghost);
  user-select: none;
  pointer-events: none;
}
.gw__inner { display: inline-block; }
.gw__dot {
  display: inline-block;
  inline-size: 0.16em;
  block-size: 0.16em;
  margin-inline-start: 0.05em;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--accent-dot);
  vertical-align: baseline;
}
/* Layer 2 — the object, centered ~22% from the start edge */
.gw__object {
  --h: clamp(420px, 62vh, 620px);
  position: absolute;
  z-index: 2;
  margin: 0;
  block-size: var(--h);
  inline-size: calc(var(--h) * var(--ar, 1));
  inset-inline-start: calc(22% - var(--h) * var(--ar, 1) / 2);
  top: calc(50% - var(--h) / 2 - 2vh);
}
.gw__pointer, .gw__pointer img { inline-size: 100%; block-size: 100%; }
.gw__pointer img { object-fit: contain; position: relative; z-index: 1; }
.gw__shadow {
  position: absolute;
  inset-inline: 18%;
  bottom: -2%;
  block-size: 7%;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: rgba(26, 36, 32, 0.18);
  filter: blur(24px);
}
/* Layer 3 — text block in the open middle area */
.gw__text {

  position: absolute;
  z-index: 3;
  inset-inline-start: 44vw;

.gw__title {
  font-weight: 800;
  font-size: clamp(64px, 6.4vw, 120px);
  line-height: 0.92;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em; /* Hebrew/Arabic; use -0.03em for Latin */

}
.gw__title > span { display: block; white-space: nowrap; }
.gw__sub {
  margin-top: 32px;
  max-width: 34ch;
  font-size: 15px;
  font-weight: 400;
  line-height: 1.65;
  color: var(--ink-muted);
}
.gw__actions { display: flex; gap: 16px; align-items: center; margin-top: 40px; }
.gw__cta, .gw__next {
  display: inline-grid;
  place-items: center;
  block-size: 54px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: var(--surface);
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.7);
  box-shadow: 10px 10px 24px rgba(160, 148, 124, 0.35), -10px -10px 24px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.95);
  text-decoration: none;
  transition: box-shadow 0.35s var(--ease), translate 0.35s var(--ease);
}
.gw__cta { padding-inline: 34px; font-size: 15px; font-weight: 500; color: var(--accent); }
.gw__next { inline-size: 54px; }
.gw__next svg { inline-size: 22px; fill: none; stroke: var(--ink-muted); stroke-width: 1.5; stroke-
linecap: round; stroke-linejoin: round; }
.gw__next:dir(ltr) svg { scale: -1 1; } /* arrow always points in the reading direction */
.gw__cta:hover, .gw__next:hover {
  translate: 0 -1px;
  box-shadow: 6px 6px 14px rgba(160, 148, 124, 0.35), -6px -6px 14px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.95);
}
.gw__cta:active, .gw__next:active {
  translate: 0 0;
  box-shadow: inset 6px 6px 12px rgba(160, 148, 124, 0.3), inset -6px -6px 12px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
}
.gw__cta:focus-visible, .gw__next:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--ink); outline-offset: 4px; }
/* Layer 1 — macro detail bleeding off the end edge and the bottom */
.gw__macro {
  --mw: clamp(260px, 26vw, 420px);
  position: absolute;
  z-index: 1;
  inline-size: var(--mw);
  block-size: auto;
  inset-inline-end: calc(var(--mw) * -0.04);
  inset-block-end: calc(var(--mw) * -0.17);
  pointer-events: none;
}
@media (min-width: 768px) and (max-width: 1023px) {
  .gw__ghost { font-size: 40vw; }
  .gw__object { --h: 50vh; inset-inline-start: calc(18% - var(--h) * var(--ar, 1) / 2); }
  .gw__text { inset-inline-start: 46vw; }

  .gw__macro { --mw: 52vw; }

@media (max-width: 380px) { .gw__title { font-size: 14vw; } }
element.js
// E1-01 · Ghost word product hero — load sequence, idle float, scroll + pointer parallax.
import { gsap, MQ } from '@/motion-core/motion.js';
export function init(root, opts = {}) {
  const o = { float: 8, pointer: 8, ghostPointer: 4, fill: 1.06, maxHeight: 0.72, ...opts };
  const $ = (s) => root.querySelector(s);
  const ghost = $('.gw__ghost'), inner = $('.gw__inner'), word = $('.gw__word'), dot = $('.gw__dot');
  const object = $('.gw__object'), pointer = $('.gw__pointer'), img = pointer.querySelector('img');
  const macro = $('.gw__macro');
  const textParts = root.querySelectorAll('.gw__title > span, .gw__sub, .gw__actions' );
  // Fit the ghost word to the hero width (any word length, any script). Mobile keeps the CSS size.
  // Short words hit the height cap first; the remaining width is filled with tracking (max 0.35em).
  const fit = () => {
    ghost.style.fontSize = '';
    ghost.style.letterSpacing = '';
    if (matchMedia('(max-width: 767px)' ).matches) return;
    const base = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(ghost).fontSize);
    const w = inner.getBoundingClientRect().width || 1;
    const targetW = root.clientWidth * o.fill;
    const size = Math.min((targetW / w) * base, root.clientHeight * o.maxHeight);
    ghost.style.fontSize = `${size}px`;
    const gap = targetW - inner.getBoundingClientRect().width;
    const letters = [...word.textContent].length;
    if (gap > 0 && letters > 1) {
      const extra = Math.min(gap / letters / size, 0.35);
      ghost.style.letterSpacing = `calc(-0.04em + ${extra.toFixed(3)}em)`;
    }
  };
  fit();
  const ro = new ResizeObserver(fit);
  ro.observe(root);
  document.fonts?.ready.then(fit);
  const mm = gsap.matchMedia();
  mm.add({ motion: MQ.motion, fine: MQ.fine }, ({ conditions }) => {
    if (!conditions.motion) return; // reduced motion: everything is already in place
    const tl = gsap.timeline({ defaults: { ease: 'power4.out' } }); // ≈  cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1)
    tl.from(ghost, { autoAlpha: 0, duration: 1.2, ease: 'power2.out' }, 0)
      .from(object, { y: 24, autoAlpha: 0, duration: 0.9 }, 0.15)
      .from(textParts, { y: 12, autoAlpha: 0, duration: 0.8, stagger: 0.08 }, 0.25)
      .from(macro, { y: 40, autoAlpha: 0, duration: 1 }, 0.3)
      .from(dot, { scale: 0, duration: 0.7, ease: 'back.out(2.2)' }, 1.1); // the only "event" on screen


## Page 32

ומוצר  רפאים  מילת· E1-01תלת־ממד  בלי:  1 טייר —  10 פרק
32
    gsap.to(img, { y: -o.float, duration: 3, ease: 'sine.inOut', yoyo: true, repeat: -1 });
    // Scroll parallax: ghost at 0.6x the scroll speed, macro at 1.15x.
    const st = { trigger: root, start: 'top top' , end: 'bottom top' , scrub: true };
    gsap.to(ghost, { y: () => root.offsetHeight * 0.4, ease: 'none', scrollTrigger: st });
    gsap.to(macro, { y: () => -root.offsetHeight * 0.15, ease: 'none', scrollTrigger: { ...st } });
    if (!conditions.fine) return;
    const px = gsap.quickTo(pointer, 'x', { duration: 0.8, ease: 'power3' });
    const py = gsap.quickTo(pointer, 'y', { duration: 0.8, ease: 'power3' });
    const gx = gsap.quickTo(inner, 'x', { duration: 1.2, ease: 'power3' });
    const gy = gsap.quickTo(inner, 'y', { duration: 1.2, ease: 'power3' });
    const move = (e) => {
      const r = root.getBoundingClientRect();
      const nx = ((e.clientX - r.left) / r.width - 0.5) * 2; // -1..1
      const ny = ((e.clientY - r.top) / r.height - 0.5) * 2;
      px(nx * o.pointer); py(ny * o.pointer);
      gx(-nx * o.ghostPointer); gy(-ny * o.ghostPointer);
    };
    root.addEventListener('pointermove', move);
    return () => root.removeEventListener('pointermove', move);
  });
  return () => { mm.revert(); ro.disconnect(); ghost.style.fontSize = ''; ghost.style.letterSpacing = ''; 
};
}

element.css
/* E1-02 · Mascot over a giant fading word. Full-bleed on desktop, floating card on mobile. */
.mw {
  --card: #63B4A6;
  --ink-on: #fff;
  --pill-ink: #1F3D38;
  --word-font: 'Anton', 'Karantina', Impact, sans-serif; /* Hebrew brand word: Karantina */
  --ar: 0.805; /* character image width / height */
  position: relative;
  inline-size: 100%;
  block-size: 100svh;
  min-block-size: 640px;
  overflow: hidden;
  isolation: isolate;
  color: var(--ink-on);
  background:
    radial-gradient(ellipse at 20% 10%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12), transparent 60%),
    var(--card);
}
.mw__word {
  position: absolute;
  z-index: 1;
  top: 14%;
  left: 50%;
  translate: -50% 0;
  margin: 0;

element.css
/* E1-04 · Video inside giant type. The page color covers the video everywhere except inside the letters.  
*/
.vt { --cover: #F1ECE4; --type-font: 'Karantina', 'Anton', Impact, sans-serif; position: relative; }
.vt__stage { position: relative; block-size: 100svh; overflow: hidden; background: #222; }
.vt__video { position: absolute; inset: 0; inline-size: 100%; block-size: 100%; object-fit: cover; }
.vt__mask { position: absolute; inset: 0; inline-size: 100%; block-size: 100%; }
.vt__cover { fill: var(--cover); }
.vt__text {
  font-family: var(--type-font);
  font-weight: 700;
  font-size: min(38vw, 64vh);
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
}
.vt__sr {
  position: absolute; inline-size: 1px; block-size: 1px; overflow: hidden;
  clip-path: inset(50%); white-space: nowrap;
}
.vt__caption {
  position: absolute;
  inset-inline-start: var(--pad, 48px);
  bottom: clamp(28px, 8vh, 80px);
  color: #fff;
  font: 400 clamp(20px, 2.2vw, 34px)/1.3 var(--font-serif, serif);
  text-shadow: 0 2px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35);
  opacity: 0;
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .vt__caption { opacity: 1; }
}
element.js
// E1-04 · Video inside type — pinned scroll zooms through the letters until the video fills the screen.
import { gsap, ScrollTrigger, MQ } from '@/motion-core/motion.js';
export function init(root, opts = {}) {
  const o = { distance: '+=160%', scale: 9, ...opts };
  const stage = root.querySelector('.vt__stage');


## Page 43

הכותרת  בתוך  וידאו· E1-04תלת־ממד  בלי:  1 טייר —  10 פרק
43
  const video = root.querySelector('.vt__video');
  const zoom = root.querySelector('.vt__zoom');
  const cover = root.querySelector('.vt__cover');
  const caption = root.querySelector('.vt__caption');
  // Play only while visible; never autoplay for reduced motion or Save-Data.
  const saveData = navigator.connection?.saveData === true;
  const io = new IntersectionObserver(([e]) => {
    if (!e.isIntersecting || saveData || matchMedia(MQ.reduce).matches) video.pause();
    else video.play().catch(() => {});
  }, { threshold: 0.1 });
  io.observe(stage);
  const mm = gsap.matchMedia();
  mm.add(MQ.motion, () => {
    const tl = gsap.timeline({
      scrollTrigger: { trigger: stage, start: 'top top' , end: o.distance, pin: true, scrub: 0.8, 
anticipatePin: 1, invalidateOnRefresh: true },
    });
    // Zoom around the text centre; the cover fades in the last third so the end is always pure video.
    tl.to(zoom, { scale: o.scale, svgOrigin: () => originFor(), ease: 'power2.in', duration: 1 }, 0)
      .to(cover, { opacity: 0, ease: 'none', duration: 0.35 }, 0.65)
      .to(caption, { opacity: 1, y: 0, ease: 'power1.out', duration: 0.25 }, 0.75);
    gsap.set(caption, { y: 20 });
    return () => gsap.set([zoom, cover, caption], { clearProps: 'all' });
  });
  // Reduced motion: show the whole video frame (poster) with the caption, no pinning.
  mm.add(MQ.reduce, () => { gsap.set(cover, { opacity: 0 }); });
  function originFor() {
    const svg = root.querySelector('.vt__mask');
    const r = svg.getBoundingClientRect();
    return `${r.width / 2} ${r.height / 2}`;
  }
  return () => { mm.revert(); io.disconnect(); ScrollTrigger.refresh(); };
}

element.css
/* E1-05 · Zoom portal: a small framed window between two words grows into the full screen on scroll. */
.zp { --bg: #F4F1EC; --ink: #1B1F24; position: relative; background: var(--bg); color: var(--ink); }
.zp__pin { position: relative; block-size: 100svh; overflow: hidden; display: grid; place-items: center; }
.zp__window {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  clip-path: inset(31% 39% 31% 39% round 22px);
  will-change: clip-path;
}
.zp__window img { inline-size: 100%; block-size: 100%; object-fit: cover; transform: scale(1.35); }
.zp__title {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 2;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 0;
  inline-size: 100%;
  font: 500 clamp(48px, 8.4vw, 150px)/1 var(--font-serif, serif);
  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
  white-space: nowrap;
  pointer-events: none;
}
.zp__gap { flex: 0 0 calc(22vw + 4vw); }
.zp__a { text-align: end; flex: 1; }
.zp__b { text-align: start; flex: 1; }
.zp__after {
  position: absolute;
  z-index: 3;
  inset-inline-start: var(--pad, 48px);
  bottom: clamp(28px, 8vh, 80px);
  color: #fff;
  opacity: 0;
}
.zp__lead { font: 400 clamp(20px, 2vw, 30px)/1.35 var(--font-serif, serif); text-shadow: 0 2px 20px 
rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35); }
.zp__cta { display: inline-block; margin-top: 18px; padding: 13px 26px; border-radius: 999px; background: 
#fff; color: var(--ink); font-weight: 600; text-decoration: none; }
@media (max-width: 767px) {
  .zp__window { clip-path: inset(32% 16% 32% 16% round 18px); }
  .zp__title { flex-direction: column; font-size: 15vw; }
  .zp__gap { flex: 0 0 40vh; }

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {

  .zp__window img { transform: none; }
  .zp__title { display: block; align-self: start; padding-top: 4vh; text-align: center; white-space: 
normal; }
  .zp__a, .zp__b { display: inline; }
  .zp__gap { display: none; }
  .zp__after { opacity: 1; color: var(--ink); }
  .zp__lead { text-shadow: none; }
  .zp__cta { background: var(--ink); color: #fff; }
}

// E1-05 · Zoom portal — pinned scroll: words part, the framed window opens to full screen.
import { gsap, MQ, isRTL } from '@/motion-core/motion.js';
export function init(root, opts = {}) {
  const o = { distance: '+=150%', ...opts };
  const pin = root.querySelector('.zp__pin');
  const win = root.querySelector('.zp__window');
  const img = win.querySelector('img');
  const a = root.querySelector('.zp__a');
  const b = root.querySelector('.zp__b');
  const after = root.querySelector('.zp__after');
  const mm = gsap.matchMedia();
  mm.add({ motion: MQ.motion, mobile: MQ.mobile }, ({ conditions }) => {
    if (!conditions.motion) return;
    const dir = isRTL(root) ? 1 : -1; // the first word sits at the inline-start side
    const out = conditions.mobile
      ? [{ y: () => -innerHeight * 0.45 }, { y: () => innerHeight * 0.45 }]
      : [{ x: () => dir * innerWidth * 0.55 }, { x: () => -dir * innerWidth * 0.55 }];
    gsap.from([a, b], { yPercent: 40, autoAlpha: 0, duration: 1.1, ease: 'power4.out', stagger: 0.08 });
    gsap.from(win, { clipPath: conditions.mobile ? 'inset(50% 50% 50% 50% round 18px)'  : 'inset(50% 50%  
50% 50% round 22px)' , duration: 1.3, ease: 'expo.out', delay: 0.15 });
    const tl = gsap.timeline({
      scrollTrigger: { trigger: pin, start: 'top top' , end: o.distance, pin: true, scrub: 0.7, 
invalidateOnRefresh: true },
      defaults: { ease: 'none' },
    });
    tl.to(win, { clipPath: 'inset(0% 0% 0% 0% round 0px)' , ease: 'power2.inOut' }, 0)
      .to(img, { scale: 1, ease: 'power2.inOut' }, 0)
      .to(a, { ...out[0], autoAlpha: 0, ease: 'power2.in' }, 0)
      .to(b, { ...out[1], autoAlpha: 0, ease: 'power2.in' }, 0)
      .to(after, { opacity: 1, duration: 0.3 }, 0.75);
    return () => gsap.set([win, img, a, b, after], { clearProps: 'all' });
  });
  return () => mm.revert();
}

element.css
/* E1-06 · Rotating word headline: one slot rolls through value words; width follows the word. */
.rw {
  --rw-line: #F2B544;
  font: 700 clamp(40px, 6.2vw, 104px)/1.08 var(--font-sans, system-ui);
  letter-spacing: -0.015em;
  max-inline-size: 14ch;
}
.rw__slot {
  position: relative;
  display: inline-block;
  vertical-align: bottom;
  block-size: 1.12em;
  overflow: hidden;
  cursor: pointer;
  padding-inline: 0.04em;
}
.rw__slot::after { /* underline that resizes with the word */
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  inset-inline: 0;
  bottom: 0.06em;
  block-size: 0.07em;
  border-radius: 1em;
  background: var(--rw-line);
}
.rw__word { display: block; white-space: nowrap; }
.rw__word[hidden] { display: none; } /* reduced motion hides the extra words; a class display would beat  
[hidden] */
.rw.is-live .rw__word { position: absolute; inset-inline-start: 0.04em; top: 0; }
.rw__sr {
  position: absolute; inline-size: 1px; block-size: 1px; overflow: hidden;
  clip-path: inset(50%); white-space: nowrap;
}

// E1-06 · Rotating word — slot-machine roll with width interpolation. Pauses on hover, tap and off-
screen.
import { gsap, MQ } from '@/motion-core/motion.js';
export function init(root, opts = {}) {
  const o = { hold: 2.2, roll: 0.75, loops: 3, ...opts }; // loops: full cycles before it settles on word  
1
  const slot = root.querySelector('.rw__slot');
  const words = [...root.querySelectorAll('.rw__word')];
  const mm = gsap.matchMedia();
  mm.add(MQ.motion, () => {
    root.classList.add('is-live');


## Page 49

מתחלפת  מילה· E1-06תלת־ממד  בלי:  1 טייר —  10 פרק
49
    const widths = () => words.map((w) => w.getBoundingClientRect().width);
    let w = widths();
    gsap.set(slot, { width: w[0] + slot.offsetHeight * 0.08 });
    gsap.set(words, { yPercent: (i) => (i ? 110 : 0) });
    const tl = gsap.timeline({ repeat: o.loops - 1, delay: o.hold, onComplete: () => tl.pause() });
    words.forEach((word, i) => {
      const next = words[(i + 1) % words.length];
      const ni = (i + 1) % words.length;
      tl.to(word, { yPercent: -110, duration: o.roll, ease: 'power4.inOut' })
        .fromTo(next, { yPercent: 110 }, { yPercent: 0, duration: o.roll, ease: 'power4.inOut', 
immediateRender: false }, '<')
        .to(slot, { width: () => w[ni] + slot.offsetHeight * 0.08, duration: o.roll, ease: 'power4.inOut' 
}, '<')
        .to({}, { duration: o.hold });
    });
    // Pauses on hover and tap, stops off-screen, and settles on word 1 after `loops` cycles.
    let manual = false;
    const pause = () => tl.pause();
    const resume = () => { if (!manual && tl.progress() < 1) tl.resume(); };
    const toggle = () => { manual = !manual; manual ? tl.pause() : resume(); };
    const io = new IntersectionObserver(([e]) => (e.isIntersecting ? resume() : pause()));
    io.observe(root);
    slot.addEventListener('pointerenter', pause);
    slot.addEventListener('pointerleave', resume);
    slot.addEventListener('click', toggle);
    const onResize = () => { w = widths(); tl.invalidate(); };
    addEventListener('resize', onResize);
    return () => {
      io.disconnect();
      slot.removeEventListener('pointerenter', pause);
      slot.removeEventListener('pointerleave', resume);
      slot.removeEventListener('click', toggle);
      removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
      root.classList.remove('is-live');
    };
  });
  // Reduced motion: the first word sits in the sentence, static.
  mm.add(MQ.reduce, () => { words.slice(1).forEach((w) => (w.hidden = true)); return () => 
words.forEach((w) => (w.hidden = false)); });
  return () => mm.revert();
}

// E1-07 · Pop-up diorama — each [data-depth] layer moves by its depth on pointer and on scroll.
import { gsap, MQ } from '@/motion-core/motion.js';
export function init(root, opts = {}) {
  const o = { pointer: 40, scroll: 260, ...opts }; // max px at depth 1
  const layers = [...root.querySelectorAll('[data-depth]')];
  const depth = (el) => Number(el.dataset.depth) || 0;
  const mm = gsap.matchMedia();
  mm.add({ motion: MQ.motion, fine: MQ.fine }, ({ conditions }) => {
    if (!conditions.motion) return;
    // Intro: layers rise from the ground like a pop-up book, back to front.
    gsap.from(layers, { yPercent: (i, el) => 8 + depth(el) * 30, autoAlpha: 0, duration: 1.4, ease: 
'power4.out', stagger: 0.07 });
    // Scroll: far layers lag behind the page (they move down relative to it), the nearest layer
    // stays glued to the page — so the bottom edge never opens a gap.
    const nearest = Math.max(...layers.map(depth));
    layers.forEach((el) => {
      gsap.to(el, {
        y: () => (nearest - depth(el)) * o.scroll,
        ease: 'none',
        scrollTrigger: { trigger: root, start: 'top top' , end: 'bottom top' , scrub: true, 
invalidateOnRefresh: true },
      });
    });
    if (!conditions.fine) return;
    // Pointer: move on x only (y belongs to scroll), using a wrapper-free quickTo on xPercent.
    const setters = layers.map((el) => gsap.quickTo(el, 'x', { duration: 1.1, ease: 'power3' }));
    const move = (e) => {
      const r = root.getBoundingClientRect();
      const nx = ((e.clientX - r.left) / r.width - 0.5) * 2;
      setters.forEach((set, i) => set(-nx * depth(layers[i]) * o.pointer));
    };
    root.addEventListener('pointermove', move);
    return () => root.removeEventListener('pointermove', move);
  });
  return () => mm.revert();
}

element.css
/* E1-08 · Mosaic assemble: scattered tiles fly into a collage grid; on scroll they drift apart. */
.mos {
  --ink: #23201C;
  --gap: clamp(8px, 1vw, 14px);
  position: relative;
  min-block-size: 100svh;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 0.9fr) minmax(0, 1.25fr);
  align-items: center;
  gap: clamp(24px, 4vw, 72px);
  padding: clamp(24px, 5vw, 80px);
  overflow: hidden;
  color: var(--ink);
}
.mos__title { font: 500 clamp(40px, 5vw, 84px)/1.02 var(--font-serif, serif); letter-spacing: -0.015em; }
.mos__lead { margin-top: 22px; max-inline-size: 34ch; font-size: 17px; color: #5d574f; }
.mos__cta { display: inline-block; margin-top: 26px; padding: 13px 26px; border-radius: 999px; background: 
var(--ink); color: #fff; font-weight: 600; text-decoration: none; }
.mos__grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
  grid-template-rows: repeat(3, clamp(120px, 24vh, 230px));
  gap: var(--gap);
}
.mos__tile { margin: 0; overflow: hidden; border-radius: 14px; background: #ddd5ca; will-change: 
transform; }
.mos__tile img { inline-size: 100%; block-size: 100%; object-fit: cover; }
.mos__tile--big { grid-column: span 2; grid-row: span 2; }
.mos__tile--tall { grid-row: span 2; }
.mos__tile--wide { grid-column: span 2; }
@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .mos { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
  .mos__grid { grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr); grid-template-rows: repeat(3, 28vw); }
  .mos__tile:nth-child(n + 6) { display: none; }
  .mos__tile--wide { grid-column: span 1; }
}

element.js
// E1-08 · Mosaic assemble — tiles fly in from random spots into the grid; drift apart on scroll.
import { gsap, MQ } from '@/motion-core/motion.js';
export function init(root, opts = {}) {
  const o = { spread: 0.45, seed: 7, ...opts };
  const tiles = [...root.querySelectorAll('.mos__tile')].filter((t) => t.offsetParent !== null);
  const text = root.querySelectorAll('.mos__title, .mos__lead, .mos__cta' );
  const mm = gsap.matchMedia();
  mm.add(MQ.motion, () => {
    // Deterministic "random" so the scatter looks designed, not chaotic, on every load.
    let s = o.seed;
    const r = (a, b) => { s = (s * 16807) % 2147483647; return a + ((s - 1) / 2147483646) * (b - a); };
    const scatter = tiles.map(() => ({ x: r(-1, 1) * innerWidth * o.spread, y: r(-1, 1) * innerHeight * 
o.spread, rotation: r(-24, 24), scale: r(0.45, 0.8) }));
    const tl = gsap.timeline({ defaults: { ease: 'expo.out' } });
    tl.from(tiles, {
      x: (i) => scatter[i].x, y: (i) => scatter[i].y, rotation: (i) => scatter[i].rotation, scale: (i) => 
scatter[i].scale,
      autoAlpha: 0, duration: 1.6, stagger: { each: 0.06, from: 'random' },
    }, 0)
      .from(text, { y: 30, autoAlpha: 0, duration: 1, stagger: 0.08, ease: 'power4.out' }, 0.4);
    // Leaving the hero: each tile drifts outward along its own scatter vector (smaller amplitude).
    tiles.forEach((t, i) => {
      gsap.to(t, {
        x: scatter[i].x * 0.35, y: scatter[i].y * 0.35 - 60, rotation: scatter[i].rotation * 0.4,
        ease: 'none',
        scrollTrigger: { trigger: root, start: 'top top' , end: 'bottom top' , scrub: 0.8 },
      });
    });
  });
  return () => mm.revert();
}

// E1-10 · Sticker board — Draggable + InertiaPlugin (free since GSAP 3.13): drag, throw, bounce off  
edges.
import { gsap, MQ } from '@/motion-core/motion.js';
import { Draggable } from 'gsap/Draggable';
import { InertiaPlugin } from 'gsap/InertiaPlugin';
gsap.registerPlugin(Draggable, InertiaPlugin);
export function init(root, opts = {}) {
  const o = { throwResistance: 1800, ...opts };
  const stickers = [...root.querySelectorAll('.stk__s')].filter((s) => s.getBoundingClientRect().width > 
0);
  let z = 10;
  const mm = gsap.matchMedia();
  mm.add(MQ.motion, () => {
    // Stickers slap onto the board one by one.
    gsap.from(stickers, { scale: 1.8, autoAlpha: 0, rotation: '+=25', duration: 0.6, ease: 
'back.out(2.4)', stagger: 0.12, delay: 0.5 });
  });
  // Dragging works in every mode (it is a user action, not autoplay motion).
  const drags = Draggable.create(stickers, {
    bounds: root,
    inertia: true,
    throwResistance: o.throwResistance,
    edgeResistance: 0.7,
    zIndexBoost: false,
    onPress() {
      this.target.style.zIndex = ++z;
      gsap.to(this.target, { scale: 1.08, duration: 0.2, overwrite: 'auto' });
    },
    onRelease() { gsap.to(this.target, { scale: 1, duration: 0.35, ease: 'back.out(3)' }); },
    onDrag() { gsap.set(this.target, { rotation: gsap.utils.clamp(-25, 25, this.deltaX * 0.8) }); },
    onThrowUpdate() { gsap.set(this.target, { rotation: gsap.utils.clamp(-25, 25, this.deltaX * 0.5) }); 
},
  });
  return () => { mm.revert(); drags.forEach((d) => d.kill()); };
}

// E1-22 · Velocity skew — every [data-skew] item leans (skewY) and stretches (scaleY) with scroll speed.
import { gsap, MQ, scrollVelocity } from '@/motion-core/motion.js';
export function init(root, opts = {}) {
  const o = { skew: 0.35, maxSkew: 9, stretch: 0.004, ...opts };
  const items = root.querySelectorAll('[data-skew]');
  const mm = gsap.matchMedia();
  mm.add(MQ.motion, () => {
    const skewTo = gsap.quickTo(items, 'skewY', { duration: 0.5, ease: 'power3' });
    const scaleTo = gsap.quickTo(items, 'scaleY', { duration: 0.5, ease: 'power3' });
    const tick = () => {
      const v = scrollVelocity();
      skewTo(gsap.utils.clamp(-o.maxSkew, o.maxSkew, v * o.skew));
      scaleTo(1 + Math.min(Math.abs(v) * o.stretch, 0.12));
    };
    gsap.ticker.add(tick);
    return () => { gsap.ticker.remove(tick); gsap.set(items, { clearProps: 'transform' }); };
  });
  return () => mm.revert();
}

element.css
/* E1-29 · 2.5D exploded view: aligned part images stacked in CSS 3D, separated by scroll. */
.ex { color: #EEF0F3; }
.ex__pin { block-size: 100svh; display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 0.8fr) minmax(0, 1.2fr); 
align-items: center; gap: 4vw; padding-inline: var(--pad, 48px); overflow: hidden; }
.ex__title { font: 500 clamp(36px, 4.4vw, 72px)/1.05 var(--font-serif, serif); }
.ex__labels { margin: 26px 0 0; padding: 0; list-style: none; display: grid; gap: 14px; counter-reset: l; 
}
.ex__labels li { font-size: 18px; color: #6F7680; padding-inline-start: 18px; border-inline-start: 2px 
solid #2A2F36; transition: color 0.4s, border-color 0.4s; }
.ex__labels li.is-on { color: #F2EFE8; border-color: #C9A24A; }
.ex__stage { perspective: 1800px; display: grid; place-items: center; }
.ex__stack { position: relative; inline-size: min(520px, 42vw); aspect-ratio: 1; transform-style: 
preserve-3d; }
.ex__layer { position: absolute; inset: 0; inline-size: 100%; block-size: 100%; object-fit: contain; }
.ex__layer--glass { opacity: 0.22; } /* no mix-blend-mode or filter inside a preserve-3d stack: Chrome  
flattens it */
@media (max-width: 767px) {
  .ex__pin { grid-template-columns: 1fr; align-content: center; gap: 2vh; }
  .ex__stack { inline-size: 78vw; }
  .ex__stage { order: -1; }
  .ex__labels { gap: 6px; margin-top: 10px; }
  .ex__labels li { font-size: 14px; }
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) { .ex__labels li { color: #F2EFE8; } }

      c.style.setProperty('--mx', `${last.x - rects[i].left}px`);
      c.style.setProperty('--my', `${last.y - rects[i].top}px`);
    });
  };
  const move = (e) => {
    if (!rects.length) measure();
    last = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };
    if (!raf) raf = requestAnimationFrame(paint);
  };
  const leave = () => { last = { x: -9999, y: -9999 }; measure(); paint(); };
  const reset = () => { rects = []; };
  grid.addEventListener('pointermove', move);
  grid.addEventListener('pointerleave', leave);
  addEventListener('scroll', reset, { passive: true });
  addEventListener('resize', reset);
  return () => {
    cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
    grid.removeEventListener('pointermove', move);
    grid.removeEventListener('pointerleave', leave);
    removeEventListener('scroll', reset);
    removeEventListener('resize', reset);
    cards.forEach((c) => { c.style.removeProperty('--mx'); c.style.removeProperty('--my'); });
  };
}

  const close = () => {
    if (active < 0) return;
    pins[active].setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    items[active].classList.remove('is-active');
    card.hidden = true;
    active = -1;
  };
  const open = (i) => {
    if (i === active) { close(); return; }
    close();
    active = i;
    pins[i].setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
    items[i].classList.add('is-active');
    if (phone()) {
      items[i].scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest', behavior: matchMedia(MQ.reduce).matches ? 'auto' : 
'smooth' });
      return;
    }
    card.innerHTML = items[i].innerHTML;
    // keep the 260px card inside the map: clamp its centre away from the edges
    const w = map.clientWidth;
    card.style.left = `${Math.min(Math.max((Number(items[i].dataset.x) / 100) * w, 142), w - 142)}px`;
    card.style.top = `${items[i].dataset.y}%`;
    card.classList.toggle('is-below', Number(items[i].dataset.y) < 40);
    card.hidden = false;
    if (!matchMedia(MQ.reduce).matches) gsap.fromTo(card, { autoAlpha: 0, scale: 0.92 }, { autoAlpha: 1, 
scale: 1, duration: 0.35, ease: 'back.out(1.8)', overwrite: true });
  };
  const onClick = (e) => {
    const pin = e.target.closest('.mp__pin');
    if (pin) { open(pins.indexOf(pin)); return; }
    if (!e.target.closest('.mp__card')) close();
  };
  const onKey = (e) => { if (e.key === 'Escape' && active > -1) { const p = pins[active]; close(); 
p.focus(); } };
  root.addEventListener('click', onClick);
  document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  const mm = gsap.matchMedia();
  mm.add(MQ.motion, () => {
    gsap.set(pins, { autoAlpha: 0 });
    gsap.set(roads, { drawSVG: '0%' });
    const stop = onceVisible(map, () => {
      gsap.timeline()
        .to(roads, { drawSVG: '100%', duration: 1.4, ease: 'power2.inOut', stagger: 0.12 })
        .fromTo(pins, { y: -70, autoAlpha: 0 }, { y: 0, autoAlpha: 1, duration: 0.9, ease: 'bounce.out', 
stagger: 0.14 }, 0.6);
    }, '0px 0px -25% 0px' );
    return () => { stop(); gsap.set([...pins, ...roads], { clearProps: 'all' }); };
  });

  const burst = (btn) => {

    const h = heart.getBoundingClientRect();
    const cx = h.left - b.left + h.width / 2;
    const cy = h.top - b.top + h.height / 2;
    const pieces = [];
    const ring = document.createElement('span');
    ring.className = 'lb__ring';
    Object.assign(ring.style, { left: `${cx}px`, top: `${cy}px` });
    btn.append(ring);
    pieces.push(ring);
    const tl = gsap.timeline({ onComplete: () => pieces.forEach((p) => p.remove()) });
    tl.fromTo(heart, { scale: 1 }, { keyframes: { scale: [0.6, 1.3, 0.92, 1] }, duration: 0.55, ease:

    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
      const dot = document.createElement('span');

      Object.assign(dot.style, { left: `${cx}px`, top: `${cy}px`, background: COLORS[i % COLORS.length] 
});
      btn.append(dot);

    }

      .fromTo(big, { scale: 0.2, opacity: 0 }, { scale: 1, opacity: 1, duration: 0.35, ease: 
'back.out(2.2)' })
      .to(big, { scale: 1.15, opacity: 0, duration: 0.35, ease: 'power2.in' }, 0.55);
  };
  const set = (btn, liked) => {
    const box = btn.querySelector('.lb__count');
    const n = Number(box?.textContent.trim()) || 0;
    btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(liked));
    if (box) roll(box, liked ? n + 1 : Math.max(0, n - 1), liked);
    if (liked && !reduce()) burst(btn);
    if (!liked && !reduce()) gsap.fromTo(btn.querySelector('.lb__heart'), { scale: 0.8 }, { scale: 1, 
duration: 0.3, ease: 'power2.out' });
    onToggle(btn, liked);
  };
  const onClick = (e) => {
    const btn = e.target.closest('.lb__like');
    if (btn && root.contains(btn)) set(btn, btn.getAttribute('aria-pressed') !== 'true');
  };
  const onDbl = (e) => {
    const media = e.target.closest('.lb__media');
    if (!media || !root.contains(media)) return;
    const card = media.closest('.lb__card');
    const btn = card.querySelector('.lb__like');
    bigHeart(card);
    if (btn.getAttribute('aria-pressed') !== 'true') set(btn, true);
  };
  root.addEventListener('click', onClick);
  root.addEventListener('dblclick', onDbl);

element.css
/* E2-01 · Product choreography: one sticky full-screen canvas above the sections draws a 3D product that  
flies
   between "anchors" (empty boxes in the layout). The posters inside the anchors are the still version. */
.pc { position: relative; isolation: isolate; }
.pc__stage {
  position: sticky; top: 0; z-index: 2; pointer-events: none;
  block-size: 100vh; block-size: 100lvh;              /* lvh: the canvas still covers the screen when the  
URL bar hides */
  margin-block-end: -100vh; margin-block-end: -100lvh; /* takes no room: the sections slide underneath */
}
.pc__canvas { display: block; inline-size: 100%; block-size: 100%; opacity: 0; transition: opacity 0.6s; }
.pc[data-gl='live'] .pc__canvas { opacity: 1; }
.pc__sec { position: relative; z-index: 1; display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; align-items: 
center; gap: clamp(24px, 4vw, 72px); min-block-size: 100svh; padding: 12vh var(--pad, 48px); overflow: 
clip; }
.pc__anchor { position: relative; justify-self: center; inline-size: min(28vw, 340px); block-size: 
min(70svh, 640px); }
.pc__poster { display: block; inline-size: 100%; block-size: 100%; object-fit: contain; rotate: var(--rz, 
0deg); transition: opacity 0.5s; }
.pc[data-gl='live'] .pc__poster { opacity: 0; }
/* floor shadow — JS sets --dock (0..1): how much the product is standing in this anchor right now */
.pc__anchor::after {
  content: ''; position: absolute; inset-inline: 16%; inset-block-end: 0; block-size: 7%; border-radius: 
50%; pointer-events: none;
  background: radial-gradient(closest-side, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4), transparent);
  opacity: var(--dock, 1);
}
/* hero: the product stands on a giant faded wordmark */
.pc__sec--hero { grid-template-columns: 1fr; grid-template-rows: 1fr auto; padding-block: 10vh 8vh; 
background: #13281E; color: #EDE9DF; }
.pc__mark {
  position: absolute; inset: 0; z-index: -1; display: grid; place-items: center; margin: 0; user-select: 
none;
  font: 700 min(42vw, 78svh)/0.8 var(--font-condensed);
  background: linear-gradient(#2B5A41, #13281E 82%); -webkit-background-clip: text; background-clip: text; 
color: transparent;
}
.pc__sec--hero .pc__anchor { block-size: min(60svh, 600px); }
.pc__bar { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: end; justify-content: space-between; gap: 20px 
40px; }
.pc__title { max-inline-size: 15ch; margin: 0; font: 800 clamp(30px, 3.6vw, 56px)/1.04 var(--font-sans); 
letter-spacing: -0.02em; text-wrap: balance; }
.pc__cta { display: inline-block; padding: 15px 30px; border-radius: 999px; background: #E3B23C; color: 
#13281E; font-weight: 800; text-decoration: none; }
.pc__cta:focus-visible { outline: 3px solid currentColor; outline-offset: 3px; }
.pc__copy { max-inline-size: 30rem; justify-self: center; }
.pc__copy h2 { margin: 0; font: 800 clamp(34px, 4.4vw, 68px)/1.02 var(--font-sans); letter-spacing: 
-0.025em; text-wrap: balance; }
.pc__copy p { margin: 18px 0 0; font-size: clamp(17px, 1.35vw, 20px); }
.pc__specs { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 14px 34px; margin: 32px 0 0; }
.pc__specs div { display: grid; gap: 2px; }
.pc__specs dt { font-size: 15px; color: #4A5E50; }
.pc__specs dd { margin: 0; font: 800 clamp(22px, 2vw, 30px)/1.15 var(--font-sans); }
.pc__sec--wall { background: #D3DCCB; color: #13281E; }
.pc__sec--wall p { color: #31453A; }
.pc__sec--lid { background: #1F1E1B; color: #EDE9DF; }
.pc__sec--lid p { color: #C9C4B8; }
.pc__sec--buy { background: #D9D4C7; color: #13281E; }
.pc__sec--buy .pc__cta { margin-top: 22px; background: #13281E; color: #EDE9DF; }
.pc__price { font: 800 clamp(40px, 4vw, 64px)/1 var(--font-sans); }

element.js
// E2-01 · Product choreography (scroll anchors) — one real 3D product (a GLB) travels down the page.
// The layout holds "anchors": empty boxes placed like images. Each one says where the product stands, how  
big it is
// (the box height) and at what angle (data-rot="x,y,z" in degrees; data-spin = whole turns on the way  
in).
// Every frame the anchors' rects are read: while an anchor is near the middle of the screen the product  
is docked in
// it (and scrolls with it); in between it flies to the next anchor — eased, with a small swoop toward the  
camera.
// One sticky full-screen canvas above the sections (pointer-events: none) draws it. A poster inside each  
anchor is
// the no-WebGL / reduced-motion version: the same layout as still images. Pointer and scroll speed tilt  
it a little.
import { gsap, MQ, scrollVelocity, clamp, lerp } from '@/motion-core/motion.js';
import { THREE, island, createRenderer, studioEnvironment, sizeToElement, loopWhenVisible, loadGLB, 
normalizeModel, worldPerPixel, disposeRenderer } from '@/motion-core/three-kit.js';
const D2R = Math.PI / 180;
const smooth = (a, b, x) => { const t = clamp((x - a) / (b - a)); return t * t * (3 - 2 * t); };
export function init(root, { model = '/assets/models/water-bottle.glb', hold = 0.22, fill = 0.9, intro = 
true } = {}) {
  const stage = root.querySelector('.pc__stage');
  const anchors = [...root.querySelectorAll('[data-pc-anchor]')];
  const isl = island(root, { minTier: 2 });
  if (!isl.ok || !anchors.length || matchMedia(MQ.reduce).matches) { isl.fail(); return isl.dispose; }
  let renderer;
  try {
    renderer = createRenderer(isl.canvas, { alpha: true, tier: isl.tier });
  } catch (err) { isl.fail(err); return isl.dispose; }
  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
  studioEnvironment(renderer, scene, 0.9);
  const key = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1.8);
  key.position.set(3, 5, 6);
  const rim = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1.4);
  rim.position.set(-5, 2, -4);
  scene.add(key, rim);
  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(20, 1, 0.1, 100); // narrow lens: little distortion away from  
centre
  camera.position.set(0, 0, 14);
  let W = 1, H = 1, wpp = 0.01;
  const stopSize = sizeToElement(renderer, camera, stage, (w, h) => { W = w; H = h; wpp = 
worldPerPixel(camera, h); });
  const poses = anchors.map((a) => {
    const [x = 0, y = 0, z = 0] = (a.dataset.rot || '0,0,0').split(',').map(Number);
    return { q: new THREE.Quaternion().setFromEuler(new THREE.Euler(x * D2R, y * D2R, z * D2R)), spin: 
Number(a.dataset.spin || 0) };
  });
  const product = new THREE.Group();

  scene.add(product);
  const q = new THREE.Quaternion(), qSpin = new THREE.Quaternion(), qTilt = new THREE.Quaternion();
  const Y = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), tiltE = new THREE.Euler();
  const drop = { p: intro ? 0 : 1 };
  const ptr = { x: 0, y: 0, tx: 0, ty: 0 };
  const dock = anchors.map(() => -1);
  let lean = 0, disposed = false, stopLoop = () => {}, introTween = null;
  const onMove = (e) => { ptr.tx = e.clientX / innerWidth - 0.5; ptr.ty = e.clientY / innerHeight - 0.5; 
};
  if (matchMedia(MQ.fine).matches) addEventListener('pointermove', onMove, { passive: true });
  const frame = (dt, t) => {
    const sr = stage.getBoundingClientRect();
    const pts = anchors.map((a) => {
      const r = a.getBoundingClientRect();
      return { x: r.left - sr.left + r.width / 2, y: r.top - sr.top + r.height / 2, h: r.height };
    });
    // which two anchors are we between? i = the one above the middle line, e = eased progress to the next
    const focus = H / 2, last = pts.length - 1;
    let i = 0, e = 0;
    if (pts[last].y <= focus) i = last;
    else if (pts[0].y < focus) {
      while (i < last - 1 && pts[i + 1].y <= focus) i++;
      e = smooth(hold, 1 - hold, (focus - pts[i].y) / (pts[i + 1].y - pts[i].y));
    }
    const j = Math.min(i + 1, last), a = pts[i], b = pts[j];
    const swoop = Math.sin(Math.PI * e), fall = 1 - drop.p;
    const x = lerp(a.x, b.x, e);
    const y = lerp(a.y, b.y, e) + Math.sin(t * 1.3) * 5 - fall * H * 0.9;
    const h = lerp(a.h, b.h, e) * fill * (1 + swoop * 0.12);
    product.position.set((x - W / 2) * wpp, -(y - H / 2) * wpp, swoop * 1.2);
    product.scale.setScalar(h * wpp); // the model is normalised to height 1
    // pose: slerp between the two anchors' angles, whole turns on the way in, a slow idle sway, the intro  
spin
    q.slerpQuaternions(poses[i].q, poses[j].q, e);
    qSpin.setFromAxisAngle(Y, (poses[j].spin * e + fall * 1.5) * Math.PI * 2 + Math.sin(t * 0.6) * 0.18);
    q.multiply(qSpin);
    ptr.x += (ptr.tx - ptr.x) * 0.06;
    ptr.y += (ptr.ty - ptr.y) * 0.06;
    lean += (clamp(scrollVelocity() * 0.004, -0.3, 0.3) - lean) * 0.08; // fast scroll: it leans, then  
settles
    qTilt.setFromEuler(tiltE.set(ptr.y * 0.3 + lean, ptr.x * 0.5, 0));
    product.quaternion.copy(qTilt).multiply(q);
    // CSS --dock (0..1) on every anchor: how much the product is "standing" there — drives the floor  
shadow
    for (let k = 0; k <= last; k++) {
      const w = k === i ? 1 - e : k === j ? e : 0;
      const v = Math.round(w * drop.p * 50) / 50;
      if (v !== dock[k]) { dock[k] = v; anchors[k].style.setProperty('--dock', v); }
    }
    renderer.render(scene, camera);
  };
  loadGLB(model).then((gltf) => {
    if (disposed) return;
    product.add(normalizeModel(gltf.scene, { height: 1 }));
    stopLoop = loopWhenVisible(root, frame);
    frame(0, 0);
    isl.live();
    if (intro) introTween = gsap.to(drop, { p: 1, duration: 1.8, ease: 'power3.out', delay: 0.25 });
  }).catch((err) => isl.fail(err));
  return () => {
    disposed = true;
    removeEventListener('pointermove', onMove);

    introTween?.kill();
    stopLoop();
    stopSize();
    anchors.forEach((a) => a.style.removeProperty('--dock'));
    disposeRenderer(renderer, scene);
    isl.dispose();
  };
}

element.css
/* E2-02 · Object stage: a 3D product on a round plinth inside a normal section — auto-turn, drag with  
inertia, arrow keys. */
.os {
  display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) minmax(0, 1.3fr); align-items: center; gap: 
clamp(24px, 4vw, 64px);
  min-block-size: 92svh; padding: 8vh var(--pad, 48px);
  background: radial-gradient(55% 75% at 70% 46%, #F2F5F6 0%, #DCE2E5 62%, #D0D8DC 100%); color: #1D2A33;
}
.os:dir(rtl) { background: radial-gradient(55% 75% at 30% 46%, #F2F5F6 0%, #DCE2E5 62%, #D0D8DC 100%); }
.os__view { position: relative; aspect-ratio: 1 / 0.92; inline-size: 100%; cursor: grab; touch-action: 
pan-y; user-select: none; -webkit-user-select: none; border-radius: 24px; }
.os__view.is-grabbing { cursor: grabbing; }
.os__view:focus-visible { outline: 3px solid #E2572B; outline-offset: -3px; }
.os__canvas, .os__poster { position: absolute; inset: 0; inline-size: 100%; block-size: 100%; }
.os__poster { padding: 10% 16% 20%; object-fit: contain; transition: opacity 0.5s; }
/* still version: a CSS plinth under the poster */
.os__view::before {
  content: ''; position: absolute; inset-inline: 17%; inset-block-end: 15%; block-size: 9%; border-radius: 
50%;
  background: #F3F5F6; box-shadow: 0 7px 0 #C9D1D6, 0 22px 30px rgba(29, 42, 51, 0.16); transition: 
opacity 0.5s;
}
.os__canvas { opacity: 0; transition: opacity 0.8s; }
.os[data-gl='live'] .os__canvas { opacity: 1; }
.os[data-gl='live'] .os__poster, .os[data-gl='live'] .os__view::before { opacity: 0; }
.os__hint {
  position: absolute; inset-block-end: 3%; inset-inline: 0; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; 
inline-size: fit-content; margin: 0 auto;
  padding: 8px 15px; border-radius: 999px; background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.72); color: #1D2A33; font-
size: 14px; font-weight: 600;
  opacity: 0; transition: opacity 0.5s; pointer-events: none;
}
.os[data-gl='live'] .os__hint { opacity: 1; transition-delay: 1.2s; }

.os__hint-touch { display: none; }
@media (pointer: coarse) { .os__hint-fine { display: none; } .os__hint-touch { display: inline; } }
.os__copy { max-inline-size: 30rem; justify-self: center; }
.os__title { margin: 0; font: 500 clamp(46px, 5.6vw, 92px)/1 var(--font-serif); letter-spacing: -0.01em; }
.os__copy p { margin: 18px 0 0; max-inline-size: 34ch; color: #44535C; font-size: clamp(17px, 1.35vw, 
20px); }
.os__dims { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 12px 30px; margin: 28px 0 0; }
.os__dims div { display: grid; }
.os__dims dt { color: #5C6B74; font-size: 14px; }
.os__dims dd { margin: 0; font-size: 19px; font-weight: 700; }

sans); }
.os__actions { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; gap: 14px 26px; margin-top: 24px; }
.os__cta { padding: 15px 28px; border-radius: 999px; background: #1D2A33; color: #F3F5F6; font-weight: 
800; text-decoration: none; }
.os__link { color: #1D2A33; font-weight: 700; text-underline-offset: 4px; }
.os__cta:focus-visible, .os__link:focus-visible { outline: 3px solid #E2572B; outline-offset: 3px; }
@media (max-width: 760px) {
  .os { grid-template-columns: 1fr; gap: 8px; padding-block: 6vh 10vh; }
  .os:dir(rtl), .os { background: radial-gradient(90% 45% at 50% 28%, #F2F5F6 0%, #DCE2E5 70%, #D0D8DC 
100%); }
  .os__view { order: -1; aspect-ratio: 1 / 1; }
  .os__copy { justify-self: stretch; }
}

// E2-02 · Object stage — the product as a real 3D object on a round plinth, inside an ordinary page  
section.
// It turns slowly by itself; a horizontal drag (mouse or finger) spins it with inertia; arrow keys turn  
it too;
// scrolling past the section adds part of a turn. Studio light without downloading an HDRI  
(RoomEnvironment), a
// contact-shadow texture instead of shadow maps. Any GLB fits: it is scaled to stand on the plinth.
// The poster (a cut-out image of the same product) is the no-WebGL version. Reduced motion: no auto-turn  
and no
// scroll turn — dragging still works (the visitor starts it).
import { MQ, ScrollTrigger, clamp } from '@/motion-core/motion.js';
import { THREE, island, createRenderer, studioEnvironment, sizeToElement, loopWhenVisible, loadGLB, 
normalizeModel, contactShadowTexture, disposeRenderer } from '@/motion-core/three-kit.js';
export function init(root, { model = '/assets/models/sheen-chair.glb', plinth = '#E9EDEF', autoSpeed = 
0.22, scrollTurn = 0.5, startYaw = -0.55 } = {}) {
  const view = root.querySelector('.os__view');
  const hint = root.querySelector('.os__hint');
  const isl = island(root, { minTier: 2 });
  if (!isl.ok) return isl.dispose;
  const reduce = matchMedia(MQ.reduce).matches;
  const auto = reduce ? 0 : autoSpeed;
  let renderer;
  try {
    renderer = createRenderer(isl.canvas, { alpha: true, tier: isl.tier });
  } catch (err) { isl.fail(err); return isl.dispose; }
  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
  studioEnvironment(renderer, scene, 0.85);
  const key = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 2);
  key.position.set(-2.5, 4, 3);
  scene.add(key);
  // spin state: yaw follows drag / inertia / auto-turn; scrollYaw eases toward the scroll target
  let yaw = startYaw, vel = auto, dragging = false, lastX = 0, lastT = 0, touched = false;


## Page 294

תלת־ממדית  מוצר  במת· E2-02תלת־ממד  רגעי:  2 טייר —  20 פרק
294
  let scrollYaw = 0, scrollTarget = 0, dirty = true, disposed = false, stopLoop = () => {};
  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(26, 1, 0.1, 50);
  const target = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.42, 0);
  const frameCamera = () => {
    // narrow (portrait) boxes: step back so the plinth still fits the width
    const dist = 6.2 / Math.min(1, camera.aspect / 1.05) ** 0.85;
    camera.position.set(0, 1.75, dist);
    camera.lookAt(target);
  };
  const stopSize = sizeToElement(renderer, camera, view, () => { frameCamera(); dirty = true; });
  const plinthMesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.1, 1.14, 0.14, 96), new 
THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: plinth, roughness: 0.6 }));
  plinthMesh.position.y = -0.07;
  const shadow = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2, 2), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: 
contactShadowTexture(256, 0.62), transparent: true, depthWrite: false }));
  shadow.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
  shadow.position.y = 0.003;
  const turntable = new THREE.Group();
  turntable.rotation.y = startYaw;
  scene.add(plinthMesh, shadow, turntable);
  const onDown = (e) => {
    if (e.button > 0) return;
    dragging = true; lastX = e.clientX; lastT = e.timeStamp; vel = 0;
    view.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
    view.classList.add('is-grabbing');
  };
  const onMove = (e) => {
    if (!dragging) return;
    const k = 6 / Math.max(320, view.clientWidth); // one full turn ≈  the width of the stage
    const dx = (e.clientX - lastX) * k;
    yaw += dx;
    vel = clamp(dx / Math.max(1 / 120, (e.timeStamp - lastT) / 1000), -9, 9);
    lastX = e.clientX; lastT = e.timeStamp; dirty = true;
    if (!touched && Math.abs(dx) > 0.02) { touched = true; hint?.classList.add('is-gone'); }
  };
  const onUp = () => { dragging = false; view.classList.remove('is-grabbing'); };
  const onKey = (e) => {
    const dir = e.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : e.key === 'ArrowLeft' ? -1 : 0;
    if (!dir) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    vel += dir * 2.4; dirty = true;
    if (!touched) { touched = true; hint?.classList.add('is-gone'); }
  };
  view.addEventListener('pointerdown', onDown);
  view.addEventListener('pointermove', onMove);
  view.addEventListener('pointerup', onUp);
  view.addEventListener('pointercancel', onUp);
  view.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  const st = reduce ? null : ScrollTrigger.create({
    trigger: root, start: 'top bottom' , end: 'bottom top' ,
    onUpdate: (self) => { scrollTarget = (self.progress - 0.5) * scrollTurn * Math.PI; },
  });
  const frame = (dt) => {
    if (!dragging) {
      vel += (auto - vel) * Math.min(1, dt * 1.5); // inertia settles back to the slow auto-turn
      yaw += vel * dt;
    }
    const ds = (scrollTarget - scrollYaw) * Math.min(1, dt * 5);
    scrollYaw += ds;
    if (!dirty && Math.abs(vel) < 1e-4 && Math.abs(ds) < 1e-5) return; // nothing moved: skip the frame
    turntable.rotation.y = yaw + scrollYaw;
    renderer.render(scene, camera);


## Page 295

תלת־ממדית  מוצר  במת· E2-02תלת־ממד  רגעי:  2 טייר —  20 פרק
295
    dirty = false;
  };
  loadGLB(model).then((gltf) => {
    if (disposed) return;
    const obj = normalizeModel(gltf.scene, { height: 1 });
    // fit any product on the plinth: footprint ≤  the plinth, then stand it on top
    const size = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(obj).getSize(new THREE.Vector3());
    const k = Math.min(1, 1.9 / Math.hypot(size.x, size.z));
    obj.scale.setScalar(k);
    obj.position.y = (size.y * k) / 2;
    turntable.add(obj);
    stopLoop = loopWhenVisible(view, frame);
    renderer.render(scene, camera);
    view.tabIndex = 0; // keyboard turning only exists in 3D
    isl.live();
  }).catch((err) => isl.fail(err));
  return () => {
    disposed = true;
    stopLoop();
    stopSize();
    st?.kill();
    view.removeEventListener('pointerdown', onDown);
    view.removeEventListener('pointermove', onMove);
    view.removeEventListener('pointerup', onUp);
    view.removeEventListener('pointercancel', onUp);
    view.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    view.removeAttribute('tabindex');
    disposeRenderer(renderer, scene);
    isl.dispose();
  };
}

element.css
/* E3-02 · Tiny planet: one sticky full-screen planet; every chapter turns a spot of it toward the camera.  
*/
.tp { position: relative; isolation: isolate; background: #BFE0F5; color: #1F2A22; }
.tp__stage { position: sticky; top: 0; z-index: 0; block-size: 100vh; block-size: 100lvh; margin-block-
end: -100vh; margin-block-end: -100lvh; pointer-events: none; }
.tp__canvas { display: block; inline-size: 100%; block-size: 100%; opacity: 0; transition: opacity 0.8s; }
.tp[data-gl='live'] .tp__canvas { opacity: 1; }
.tp__ch { position: relative; z-index: 1; display: flex; align-items: center; min-block-size: 130svh; 
padding: 0 var(--pad, 48px); }
.tp__ch:nth-of-type(even) { justify-content: flex-end; }
.tp__ch:first-of-type { min-block-size: 100svh; } /* the opening card must be on the first screen */
/* still version (no WebGL / reduced motion): each chapter shows a still of its shot */
.tp:not([data-gl='live']) .tp__ch { min-block-size: 100svh; background: var(--poster) center / cover no-
repeat; }
.tp__card {

@media (max-width: 760px) {

}
element.js
// E3-02 · Tiny planet — the whole place on one small, low-poly planet. Each chapter turns the planet so  
its spot
// (vineyard, lake, mountain, cabin…) comes up to face the camera, which glides in close; the first  
chapter shows
// the whole planet spinning slowly. The day passes with the story: the last chapter is dusk — the sky  
darkens,
// stars come out and the cabin windows glow. Spots are data on the chapters (data-lat / data-lon).
// The demo planet is built in code from a handful of primitives (flat-shaded); swap buildPlanet() for  
your own.
// Draws only when something moves. No WebGL / weak device / reduced motion: a still per chapter (data-
poster).
import { MQ } from '@/motion-core/motion.js';
import { THREE, island, createRenderer, sizeToElement, loopWhenVisible, chapterPosition, disposeRenderer } 
from '@/motion-core/three-kit.js';
const R = 3;
const D2R = Math.PI / 180;
const Y = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0);
const flat = (c, extra = {}) => new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: c, roughness: 0.9, flatShading: 
true, ...extra });
export const normalAt = (lat, lon) => new THREE.Vector3(Math.cos(lat * D2R) * Math.sin(lon * D2R), 
Math.sin(lat * D2R), Math.cos(lat * D2R) * Math.cos(lon * D2R));
// stand an object on the surface at (lat, lon), its +Y along the local up, turned by `yaw` around it
function plant(planet, obj, lat, lon, yaw = 0, lift = 0) {
  const n = normalAt(lat, lon);
  obj.position.copy(n).multiplyScalar(R + lift);
  obj.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(Y, n).multiply(new THREE.Quaternion().setFromAxisAngle(Y, yaw));
  planet.add(obj);
  return obj;
}
const tree = (s = 1) => {
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  const trunk = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.03 * s, 0.045 * s, 0.22 * s, 5), 
flat('#7A5A3C'));
  trunk.position.y = 0.11 * s;
  const crown = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(0.16 * s, 0), flat(['#6E9152', '#5E8447', 
'#7FA05C'][Math.floor(Math.random() * 3)]));
  crown.position.y = 0.3 * s;
  g.add(trunk, crown);
  return g;
};
const house = (wall, roof, lit = false) => {
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.34, 0.24, 0.28), flat(wall));


## Page 417

קטן  לכת  כוכב· E3-02תלת־ממד  כולו:  3 טייר —  30 פרק
417
  body.position.y = 0.12;
  const top = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.28, 0.18, 4), flat(roof));
  top.position.y = 0.33; top.rotation.y = Math.PI / 4;
  g.add(body, top);
  if (lit) { // windows that glow at dusk (emissive intensity is animated)
    const win = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.08, 0.07, 0.01), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ 
color: '#2A1E14', emissive: '#FFB45C', emissiveIntensity: 0 }));
    win.position.set(-0.07, 0.13, 0.141);
    const win2 = win.clone(); win2.position.x = 0.07;
    g.add(win, win2);
    g.userData.windows = win.material;
  }
  return g;
};
function buildPlanet(scene) {
  const planet = new THREE.Group();
  planet.add(new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(R, 5), flat('#A7C57E')));
  // the lake (a cap of the sphere, a hair above the ground) with a small boat
  const lake = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(R + 0.012, 48, 6, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, 0.36), 
flat('#5BA7C9', { roughness: 0.25, flatShading: false }));
  lake.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(Y, normalAt(-15, 80));
  planet.add(lake);
  const boat = new THREE.Group();
  const hull = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.22, 0.05, 0.08), flat('#F2EBDD')); hull.position.y = 
0.03;
  const sail = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.06, 0.18, 3), flat('#FFFFFF')); sail.position.y = 
0.14;
  boat.add(hull, sail);
  plant(planet, boat, -13, 76, 0.6, 0.012);
  // vineyard: rows of small round vines, and the winery
  for (let r = 0; r < 6; r++) for (let c = 0; c < 9; c++) {
    const vine = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(0.055, 0), flat('#4F7A36'));
    vine.position.y = 0.05;
    const g = new THREE.Group(); g.add(vine);
    plant(planet, g, 14 + r * 2.4, -9 + c * 2.1);
  }
  plant(planet, house('#EADCC5', '#8E4B32'), 24, 12, 0.3);
  // the mountain, with snow on top (a real landmark makes the planet "local")
  const mount = new THREE.Group();
  const cone = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.85, 1.1, 7), flat('#8A9A72')); cone.position.y = 
0.45;
  const snow = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.33, 0.43, 7), flat('#F6F4EF')); snow.position.y = 
0.795;
  mount.add(cone, snow);
  plant(planet, mount, 34, 168);
  // the cabin among trees
  const cabin = plant(planet, house('#B98A5C', '#5A3A28', true), -26, -98, -0.4);
  cabin.scale.setScalar(1.7);
  const glow = new THREE.PointLight('#FFB45C', 0, 1.6, 1.5); // warm light spilling from the cabin at dusk
  glow.position.set(0, 0.2, 0.3);
  cabin.add(glow);
  cabin.userData.glow = glow;
  // trees everywhere except on the spots
  const spots = [[-15, 80, 26], [19, 0, 18], [34, 168, 16], [-26, -98, 6]];
  let placed = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < 900 && placed < 140; i++) {
    const lat = Math.asin(Math.random() * 2 - 1) / D2R, lon = Math.random() * 360 - 180;
    const n = normalAt(lat, lon);
    if (spots.some(([a, b, r]) => n.angleTo(normalAt(a, b)) < r * D2R)) continue;
    plant(planet, tree(0.7 + Math.random() * 0.7), lat, lon, Math.random() * 6);
    placed++;
  }
  planet.traverse((o) => { if (o.isMesh) { o.castShadow = true; o.receiveShadow = true; } });
  scene.add(planet);


## Page 418

קטן  לכת  כוכב· E3-02תלת־ממד  כולו:  3 טייר —  30 פרק
418
  // clouds orbit on their own
  const clouds = new THREE.Group();
  for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
    const c = new THREE.Group();
    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
      const puff = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(0.16 + Math.random() * 0.08, 0), 
flat('#FFFFFF'));
      puff.position.set(k * 0.17 - 0.17, Math.random() * 0.05, Math.random() * 0.08);
      c.add(puff);
    }
    const n = normalAt(Math.random() * 120 - 60, i * 51);
    c.position.copy(n).multiplyScalar(R + 0.9 + Math.random() * 0.4);
    c.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(Y, n);
    clouds.add(c);
  }
  scene.add(clouds);
  return { planet, clouds, windows: cabin.userData.windows, glow: cabin.userData.glow };
}
export function init(root, { face = [0, 0.82, 0.57] } = {}) {
  const chapters = [...root.querySelectorAll('[data-lat]')];
  const isl = island(root, { minTier: 2 });
  if (!isl.ok || !chapters.length || matchMedia(MQ.reduce).matches) { isl.fail(); return isl.dispose; }
  let renderer;
  try {
    renderer = createRenderer(isl.canvas, { alpha: false, tier: isl.tier });
  } catch (err) { isl.fail(err); return isl.dispose; }
  renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;
  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
  const { planet, clouds, windows, glow } = buildPlanet(scene);
  const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#DDEBFF', '#7C8B5C', 1.2);
  const sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight('#FFF3DF', 2.6);
  sun.position.set(-6, 9, 7);
  sun.castShadow = true;
  sun.shadow.mapSize.set(isl.tier >= 3 ? 2048 : 1024, isl.tier >= 3 ? 2048 : 1024);
  Object.assign(sun.shadow.camera, { left: -5, right: 5, top: 5, bottom: -5, far: 30 });
  sun.shadow.bias = -0.0006;
  scene.add(hemi, sun);
  // stars for the dusk chapter
  const starGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  const sp = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 700; i++) { const v = new THREE.Vector3().randomDirection().multiplyScalar(40 + 
Math.random() * 20); sp.push(v.x, v.y, v.z); }
  starGeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(sp, 3));
  const stars = new THREE.Points(starGeo, new THREE.PointsMaterial({ color: '#FFFFFF', size: 0.12, 
transparent: true, opacity: 0 }));
  scene.add(stars);
  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(32, 1, 0.1, 120);
  const side = getComputedStyle(root).direction === 'rtl' ? 1 : -1; // first card sits at the start side
  let dirty = true;
  const stopSize = sizeToElement(renderer, camera, isl.canvas, (w, h) => { camera.userData.portrait = h > 
w; dirty = true; });
  // each chapter's spot →  the planet orientation that turns that spot toward `face` (up and toward the  
camera)
  const F = new THREE.Vector3(...face).normalize();
  const turns = chapters.map((c) => new 
THREE.Quaternion().setFromUnitVectors(normalAt(Number(c.dataset.lat), Number(c.dataset.lon)), F));
  const day = [new THREE.Color('#BFE0F5'), new THREE.Color('#C9E2F2'), new THREE.Color('#D6E0EE'), new 
THREE.Color('#E9D7C9'), new THREE.Color('#2C3258')];
  const bg = new THREE.Color(), q = new THREE.Quaternion(), spin = new THREE.Quaternion(), look = new 
THREE.Vector3();
  let lastP = -1, lastT = 0;


## Page 419

קטן  לכת  כוכב· E3-02תלת־ממד  כולו:  3 טייר —  30 פרק
419
  const frame = (dt, t) => {
    const p = chapterPosition(chapters);
    const moving = Math.abs(p - lastP) > 1e-4;
    if (!moving && !dirty && t - lastT < 1 / 30) return; // idle: 30 fps is plenty for slow clouds
    lastT = t; lastP = p; dirty = false;
    const n = chapters.length - 1, k = Math.min(Math.floor(p), n - 1), f = p - k;
    const e = f * f * (3 - 2 * f);
    q.slerpQuaternions(turns[k], turns[k + 1] || turns[k], e);
    // the opening chapter: the whole planet turns slowly; the closer we get to chapter 2, the more it  
settles
    const idle = Math.max(0, 1 - p) * t * 0.12;
    spin.setFromAxisAngle(F, idle);
    planet.quaternion.copy(spin).multiply(q);
    clouds.rotation.y = t * 0.03;
    // camera: far for the overview, close for the spots
    const near = Math.min(1, p);
    const portrait = camera.userData.portrait;
    const far = portrait ? 21 : 16, close = portrait ? 12.5 : 8.6;
    // the planet steps aside from the text card, which alternates sides chapter by chapter
    const dx = portrait ? 0 : Math.cos(Math.PI * p) * (1.9 - near * 0.35) * side;
    camera.position.set(dx, 1 + near * 4.8, far + (close - far) * near);
    look.set(dx, near * 2.35, 0);
    camera.lookAt(look);
    // the day: the last chapter turns to dusk
    const dusk = Math.max(0, p - (n - 1));
    bg.copy(day[k]).lerp(day[k + 1] || day[k], e);
    scene.background = bg;
    sun.intensity = 2.6 - dusk * 2.1;
    hemi.intensity = 1.2 - dusk * 0.75;
    stars.material.opacity = dusk;
    windows.emissiveIntensity = dusk * 3;
    glow.intensity = dusk * 2.5;
    renderer.render(scene, camera);
  };
  const stopLoop = loopWhenVisible(root, frame);
  frame(0, 0);
  isl.live();
  return () => {
    stopLoop();
    stopSize();
    disposeRenderer(renderer, scene);
    isl.dispose();
  };
}

/**
 * GLSL helpers shared by the shader elements: snoise(vec2), snoise(vec3) (Ashima simplex noise, MIT),
 * fbm2(vec2) and hash12(vec2). Paste with ${GLSL_NOISE} inside a fragment/vertex shader string.
 */
export const GLSL_NOISE = /* glsl */  `
vec3 mod289(vec3 x){ return x - floor(x * (1.0 / 289.0)) * 289.0; }
vec4 mod289(vec4 x){ return x - floor(x * (1.0 / 289.0)) * 289.0; }
vec2 mod289(vec2 x){ return x - floor(x * (1.0 / 289.0)) * 289.0; }
vec3 permute(vec3 x){ return mod289(((x * 34.0) + 10.0) * x); }
vec4 permute(vec4 x){ return mod289(((x * 34.0) + 10.0) * x); }
vec4 taylorInvSqrt(vec4 r){ return 1.79284291400159 - 0.85373472095314 * r; }
float snoise(vec2 v){
  const vec4 C = vec4(0.211324865405187, 0.366025403784439, -0.577350269189626, 0.024390243902439);
  vec2 i = floor(v + dot(v, C.yy)); vec2 x0 = v - i + dot(i, C.xx);
  vec2 i1 = (x0.x > x0.y) ? vec2(1.0, 0.0) : vec2(0.0, 1.0);
  vec4 x12 = x0.xyxy + C.xxzz; x12.xy -= i1; i = mod289(i);
  vec3 p = permute(permute(i.y + vec3(0.0, i1.y, 1.0)) + i.x + vec3(0.0, i1.x, 1.0));
  vec3 m = max(0.5 - vec3(dot(x0, x0), dot(x12.xy, x12.xy), dot(x12.zw, x12.zw)), 0.0); m = m * m; m = m *  
m;
  vec3 x = 2.0 * fract(p * C.www) - 1.0; vec3 h = abs(x) - 0.5; vec3 ox = floor(x + 0.5); vec3 a0 = x -  
ox;
  m *= 1.79284291400159 - 0.85373472095314 * (a0 * a0 + h * h);
  vec3 g; g.x = a0.x * x0.x + h.x * x0.y; g.yz = a0.yz * x12.xz + h.yz * x12.yw;
  return 130.0 * dot(m, g);
}
float snoise(vec3 v){
  const vec2 C = vec2(1.0 / 6.0, 1.0 / 3.0); const vec4 D = vec4(0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0);
  vec3 i = floor(v + dot(v, C.yyy)); vec3 x0 = v - i + dot(i, C.xxx);
  vec3 g = step(x0.yzx, x0.xyz); vec3 l = 1.0 - g; vec3 i1 = min(g.xyz, l.zxy); vec3 i2 = max(g.xyz,  
l.zxy);
  vec3 x1 = x0 - i1 + C.xxx; vec3 x2 = x0 - i2 + C.yyy; vec3 x3 = x0 - D.yyy; i = mod289(i);
  vec4 p = permute(permute(permute(i.z + vec4(0.0, i1.z, i2.z, 1.0)) + i.y + vec4(0.0, i1.y, i2.y, 1.0)) +  
i.x + vec4(0.0, i1.x, i2.x, 1.0));
  float n_ = 0.142857142857; vec3 ns = n_ * D.wyz - D.xzx;
  vec4 j = p - 49.0 * floor(p * ns.z * ns.z); vec4 x_ = floor(j * ns.z); vec4 y_ = floor(j - 7.0 * x_);
  vec4 x = x_ * ns.x + ns.yyyy; vec4 y = y_ * ns.x + ns.yyyy; vec4 h = 1.0 - abs(x) - abs(y);
  vec4 b0 = vec4(x.xy, y.xy); vec4 b1 = vec4(x.zw, y.zw);
  vec4 s0 = floor(b0) * 2.0 + 1.0; vec4 s1 = floor(b1) * 2.0 + 1.0; vec4 sh = -step(h, vec4(0.0));
  vec4 a0 = b0.xzyw + s0.xzyw * sh.xxyy; vec4 a1 = b1.xzyw + s1.xzyw * sh.zzww;
  vec3 p0 = vec3(a0.xy, h.x); vec3 p1 = vec3(a0.zw, h.y); vec3 p2 = vec3(a1.xy, h.z); vec3 p3 =  
vec3(a1.zw, h.w);
  vec4 norm = taylorInvSqrt(vec4(dot(p0, p0), dot(p1, p1), dot(p2, p2), dot(p3, p3)));
  p0 *= norm.x; p1 *= norm.y; p2 *= norm.z; p3 *= norm.w;
  vec4 m = max(0.5 - vec4(dot(x0, x0), dot(x1, x1), dot(x2, x2), dot(x3, x3)), 0.0); m = m * m;
  return 105.0 * dot(m * m, vec4(dot(p0, x0), dot(p1, x1), dot(p2, x2), dot(p3, x3)));
}
float fbm2(vec2 p){ float a = 0.5, s = 0.0; for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) { s += a * snoise(p); p = p * 2.03  
+ 17.1; a *= 0.5; } return s; }
float hash12(vec2 p){ vec3 p3 = fract(vec3(p.xyx) * 0.1031); p3 += dot(p3, p3.yzx + 33.33); return  
fract((p3.x + p3.y) * p3.z); }
`;
const texLoader = new THREE.TextureLoader();
/** Loads an image as an sRGB texture (mipmapped, anisotropic). Resolves to the texture; .image has the  
size. */
export function loadTexture(url, { colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace, anisotropy = 4 } = {}) {
  return texLoader.loadAsync(url).then((t) => { t.colorSpace = colorSpace; t.anisotropy = anisotropy; 
return t; });
